# Crop Growth Calendars

This page lists crop growth tables for all [crops](/Crops "Crops"). The "Base" table shows growth without the [Agriculturist](/Skills#Farming "Skills") profession and without any growth fertilizer. Below each Base table, comparisons between growth fertilizers and the Agriculturist Profession are shown. Note that the increase in growth is 10% with [Speed-Gro](/Speed-Gro "Speed-Gro") *or* the Agriculturist Profession, so the "10%" table applies to each.

With the exception of multi-harvest crops, the tables assume that seeds are replanted and watered on the day of harvest.

## Other Crops

### [Ancient Fruit](/Ancient_Fruit "Ancient Fruit")

If planted on Day 1 of a season, the first harvest will occur on Day 1 of the following season. Growth fertilizers affect only the first harvest.
